But we can still manage this on an operational level. At this point. Let's go ahead and create our first iMachining toolpath. So there is 2 and 3D iMachining. 2D is great at prismatic features. While 3D can take complex three dimensional shapes and rough them out. We'll be using is just the 2D iMachining. Now it's very easy to set up. We just have to select the face we want to cut. Move on to the tool. Select that tool. Now when we are creating a tool, it is very important that we want to make this accurately to what tool will actually be using.
